      return "cors:" + pe.r.cors + ",no-cors:" + pe.r.nocors;
    };
    var vprobe = function (url, done) {
      if (typeof fetch !== "function") return done();
      var cache = vprobeCache();
      var pe = cache[url];
      if (pe) {
        if (pe.left) pe.wait.push(done);
        else done();
        return;
      }
      pe = cache[url] = { r: { cors: "timeout", nocors: "timeout" }, left: 2, wait: [done] };
      var fin = function (key, val) {
        pe.r[key] = val;
        if (--pe.left) return;
        var w = pe.wait;
        pe.wait = [];
        for (var pw = 0; pw < w.length; pw++) w[pw]();
      };
      var one = function (mode, key) {
        try {
          fetch(url, { mode: mode, cache: "no-store", credentials: "omit" }).then(function (res) {
            fin(key, mode === "no-cors" ? "reached" : res.ok ? "ok" : "http" + res.status);
          }, function () {
            fin(key, "fail");
          });
        } catch (e) {
          fin(key, "fail");
        }
      };
      one("cors", "cors");
      one("no-cors", "nocors");
    };
    
    var vgiveup = function (ids, vkd) {
      vthrew = true;
      var vdur = Date.now() - vt0;
      var vonline = typeof navigator !== "undefined" && typeof navigator.onLine === "boolean" ? String(navigator.onLine) : "na";
      var vurls = [];
      for (var vl = 0; vl < ids.length; vl++) vurls.push(vpaths[ids[vl]] + ".js");
      var vleft = ids.length;
      var vsent = false;
      
      var vthrow = function () {
        if (vsent) return;
        vsent = true;
        var vmsg = [];
        for (var vd = 0; vd < ids.length; vd++) {
          vmsg.push(ids[vd] + " (" + vkd + ") " + vurls[vd] + " | try=" + (1 + vinfo(ids[vd]).tries) + " dur=" + vdur + "ms online=" + vonline + " probe=" + vprobeText(vurls[vd]));
        }
        
        setTimeout(function () {
          throw new Error("[Fender] shared vendor load failed: " + vmsg.join("; "));
        }, 0);
      };
      
      setTimeout(vthrow, 3000);
      for (var vq = 0; vq < vurls.length; vq++) {
        vprobe(vurls[vq], function () { if (--vleft === 0) vthrow(); });
      }
    };
    
    
    var vpend = {};
    var vretry = function (id) {
      vpend[id] = 0;
      if (vran || vthrew) return;
      var s = vinfo(id);
      
      if (!s.busy && s.n < vdelays.length) {
        s.n++;
        s.tries++;
        s.busy = 1;
        vreq.undef(id);
      }
      vreq(vids, vrun, vfail);
    };
    var vlater = function (id, ms) {
      vpend[id] = 1;
      setTimeout(function () { vretry(id); }, ms);
    };
    
    var vfail = function (err, kind) {
      if (vran || vthrew) return;
      var vkd = kind || (err && err.requireType) || "error";
      var ids = (err && err.requireModules) || vids;
      var dead = typeof vreq.undef !== "function";
      for (var vk = 0; vk < ids.length; vk++) {
        var s = vinfo(ids[vk]);
        
        s.busy = 0;
        
        var late = !vseen[ids[vk]] && s.n >= vdelays.length;
        vseen[ids[vk]] = 1;
        if (late) s.n = vdelays.length - 1;
        if (s.n >= vdelays.length) {
          dead = true;
          continue;
        }
        if (!vpend[ids[vk]]) vlater(ids[vk], late ? 0 : vdelays[s.n]);
      }
      if (dead) return vgiveup(ids, vkd);
    };
    vreq(vids, vrun, vfail);
  };

      try { return fn(); }
      catch (e) { if (!fenderIsMismatch(e) || attempt >= 4) throw e; }

        var s = vinfo(ids[vk]);
        
        s.busy = 0;
        
        var late = !vseen[ids[vk]] && s.n >= vdelays.length;
        vseen[ids[vk]] = 1;
        if (late) s.n = vdelays.length - 1;
        if (s.n >= vdelays.length) {
          dead = true;
          continue;
        }
        if (!vpend[ids[vk]]) vlater(ids[vk], late ? 0 : vdelays[s.n]);
      }
      if (dead) return vgiveup(ids, vkd);
    };
    vreq(vids, vrun, vfail);
  };

  var fenderRetryMismatch = function (fn) {
    
    for (var attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
      try { return fn(); }
      catch (e) { if (!fenderIsMismatch(e) || attempt >= 4) throw e; }
    }
  };
  var fenderSyncTick = function (fn) { fn(); };
  var fenderConfig = function (cfg) {
    var rq = window.require;
    var syncContext = function () {
      try {
        var ctx = rq.s && rq.s.contexts && rq.s.contexts[cfg.context];
        if (ctx && typeof ctx.nextTick === "function") ctx.nextTick = fenderSyncTick;
      } catch (e) {}
    };
    
    syncContext();
    var tick = rq.nextTick;
    var r;
    try {
      if (typeof tick === "function") rq.nextTick = fenderSyncTick;
      r = fenderRetryMismatch(function () { return rq.config(cfg); });
    } finally {
      if (typeof tick === "function") rq.nextTick = tick;

    var call = function (ids, ok, ng) {
      return fenderRetryMismatch(function () { return r(ids, ok, ng); });
    };

  };

          });
        } catch (e) {
          fin(key, "fail");
        }
      };

      vthrew = true;

    };

    };
    var vlater = function (id, ms) {
      vpend[id] = 1;
      setTimeout(function () { vretry(id); }, ms);
    };
    
    var vfail = function (err, kind) {

      var ids = (err && err.requireModules) || vids;

          continue;
        }

      }
      if (dead) return vgiveup(ids, vkd);
    };
    vreq(vids, vrun, vfail);
